# Decision-clock recalibration — 2026-10-18

Coordinator decisions: level IR = M/N segment heads, tails excluded; absolute prediction error <=0.005. Latency-weighted IR remains a separate measured metric. Q3 reproduces the original empirical successful-library LOEO q90 residual / q10 advance procedure, with no new online FAR/coverage guarantee.

Status: **APPLIED_WITH_EXCEPTIONS**; 840 potential level definitions, 42 distinct C10 Q3 artifacts. 2 levels outside tolerance are explicitly listed below. Full/reduced M/N solutions share an artifact when their library/metric and selected episodes agree; denoising compute is deliberately not a factor in the proxy.

## Original procedure and decision-clock replay

`cache_knob.calibrate` / r17_ops/calibrate_knob.py used B-only R11 gap-cap replay, vision=0/miss=1, bracket [0,40], 26 bisections and four-decimal gaps. Old replay counted native slots and let periodic looks interrupt tails. The new replay selects head rows at step % (commit/replan) ==0, restarts the same task/init-keyed hash at head zero, and counts only heads. Confirmed Q3 model calls, its one-anchor cooldown and empty-LOEO MISS are included in M. Knob and Q3 MISS both reset the accepted model-call history, exactly as serving does. The vector replay is checked against literal `periodic()` with the same forced-call masks. No test/development evaluation outcomes enter fitting.

The full inventory contains k50/k40/k30/k20/k10, koff and non-periodic chg/chrg cohorts. There are no additional ir-named or other periodic levels outside this fitted ladder.

The Q3 fitter receives those successful head rows with H=1, exec_steps=1, commit_controls=1: one committed segment is its temporal unit. It uses the original fit implementation, inverse ECDFs, W=max(2,ceil(.05*Tmed)), K=min(5,E-1), q90/q10 and minimum four reference episodes. The complete deployed metric is attached byte-for-byte for the standard serving attestation; calibration uses only its head rows. No PCA, whitening, retrieval metric or corrector is fitted. Old artifacts remain in place; new directories end `_decision1018`, and per-run aliases live under assets/q3_decision1018.

## Explicit tolerance exceptions

- fa_ls_dp_r_u_l_l / libero_spatial/100/k10: best 0.1059289, nominal 0.10, gap 39.999, off floor 0.1059289
- fa_ls_dp_f_u_l_l / libero_spatial/100/k10: best 0.1059289, nominal 0.10, gap 39.999, off floor 0.1059289

Both exceptions have M/N already above .105 with periodic disabled: 134/1265 = .1059288538, imposed by the unchanged confirmed-Q3 call rule after empirical recalibration. No periodic gap can reduce those calls. They remain explicitly failed in the solution map and deferred template metadata; neither the nominal target nor Q3 is relaxed. They may be absent from dispatch if the cell passes the formal OFF rule.

## Definitions, identities and deferred builds

Only never-prepared recalibration-dependent definitions are regenerated in place. Their old YAML/JSON/plan files are renamed aside. `task_namespace` retains each old namespace hash while actual new YAML, recipe and bundle identities remain in serving/evidence metadata; UID, init, seed, sampler, serving configuration and topology hashes are verified unchanged. The optional namespace branch is pinned into the frozen driver evidence overlay. Deferred LIBERO levels apply the same calibrated input map after the new OFF cell rule, preserving their original scientific namespace.

The six independent cohorts already moved before the gate stay byte-identical: `fa_ls_v04_1`, `fa_ls_v04_2`, `fa_ls_v05_1`, `fa_ls_v05_2`, `fa_ls_v05_3`, `fa_ls_v14_0` (3,287 episodes). The other 38 built gated campaigns and eight deferred units depend on recalibration. Native pure/pure+long, Cosmos and legacy R15/R16 VLA stay unchanged. No chain or release line is written.

## CPU checks

See RECALIBRATION_TESTS.final.log, RECALIBRATION_CPU_PROOF.json, RECALIBRATION_DEFERRED_CPU_PROOF.json and RECALIBRATION_APPLIED.json records. No server, worker, driver, simulator or GPU job was launched.

Final verification: 60/60 frozen startup/planner checks; 8/8 deferred template checks; 35 targeted tests. CUDA initialized: 0. Maximum actual GPU-hash MPS pipe: 52 bytes (limit 85). Descriptor equality is bound to the existing strict frozen-loader proof; the new bootstrap and planner are exercised in the same sealed-version interpreter. Immutable multi-GB weights are not reloaded on the busy data disk. See RECALIBRATION_FINAL_VERIFICATION.json for unchanged UID/init/seed, C5 bytes, full-metric and original-artifact checks.

Pre-existing source metadata drift (unchanged from the initial CPU proof): `fa_ls_fw_f_u_o_l`, `fa_ls_fw_f_f_o_l`, `fa_ls_fw_f_p_l`, `fa_ls_fw_f_b_l`. The inherited Fast-WAM formal-batch code seal is record-only; its old sealed spec differs from the actual source ready identity. The exact model descriptors, native loader pass, package pins and source actual identity remain equal, as recorded by the original strict CPU receipts. New full-step pure/native-long controls use that same serving environment. No loader check is weakened here.
